Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in recognition theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 784 and the invariant I(A,B) = 941 mod 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 784 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the recognition structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 941. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
